Code QUALITY → quality-bar. Even a one-line leaf fix runs --edit-check. ([aipeople11/codecortex](https://github.com/aipeople11/codecortex/tree/HEAD/skills/codecortex-change-check) · ★1)
- **codecortex-find-bug**: You have a SYMPTOM — crash, exception, wrong output, failing test, error string — and don't know which code is responsible. ([aipeople11/codecortex](https://github.com/aipeople11/codecortex/tree/HEAD/skills/codecortex-find-bug) · ★1)
